投資評等  強力買進

士電(1503)  
目標價位  250 元

獲利成長至 2028 年無虞

公司治理評鑑排序： 
1 2 3 4 5 6 7

個股基本資料：

徐培勛 
鋼鐵、重電產業 
S1120023@tss-c.com.tw 
 股價評價基礎： 
AI、半導體帶動用電需求激增，士電已接獲客戶需求，重電 T4 廠產能將於
4Q27 量產 ，2H28 開始貢獻營收 ；2027~2028 進入表後儲能工程認列高峰 ，
預估 2026/2027/2028 稅後 淨利 YoY+29%/+33%/+15% ， 稅 後EPS 
8.10/10.80/12.46 元，給予強力買進評等，目標價 250 元(20x 2028 EPS)，
潛在漲幅 22%。

營運近況摘要： 
士電成立於 1955 年，為台灣老牌變壓器生產商，技術來自三菱重工的技轉，
1H26 產品組合：電力配電(變壓器、配電盤)72%、車輛零件 15%、自動化設
備(工具機)10%、其他 3%。內外銷比 65%：35%。客戶包含：Mitsubishi 
Electric/Mitsuba Electric/ Panasonic/ Hitachi ABB/ Siemens/泓德能源/起
而行/和泰車。目前三個廠區都在新竹：電裝廠、新豐廠及重電廠。

2026 年重電 T1+T2 廠產能利用率提升，T3 廠、表後儲能小幅貢獻 
預估 2026 年營收 415 億元(YoY+12%)，因變壓器需求殷切，重電 T1、T2
廠產能利用率提升至 90%，重電 T3 廠 2025/9 開始量產，Lead time 1~1.5
年，4Q26 開始認列營收，2027 年全面貢獻；且公司在手 500MW 表後儲能
(太陽能儲能)統包案也開始貢獻營收 ； 毛利率21%(YoY+0.2ppts)， 因產品組
合較同期相同； 業外則有 金融資產 公允價值、匯兌利益挹注，稅後淨利
YoY+29%，稅後 EPS 8.15 元。

2027~2028 年 T3、T4 廠產能提升，表後儲能進入認列高峰 
展望 2027~2028 年，重電內銷(佔重電 80%)：政府希望強韌電網計畫完工
期限從 2033 年提早至 2028 年，依 2027 年預算書依然維持 2033 年完工，
2026/2027/2028 資本支出 46/118/155 億元，維持成長態勢。重電外銷(佔
重電 20%)： 為了因應全球 AI、半導體等強勁用電需求，重電 T4 廠將於
2027/9 量產，預期2H28 開始貢獻營收，2029 年全面貢獻，T1+T2 年產能
10GVA(十億伏安)，T3 可貢獻產能 5GVA、T4 再貢獻 5GVA，T4 主要客戶
為日本、美國電力市場，並專注在 345KV 變壓器生產。國內表後儲能(太陽
能)：公司在手 500MW 表後儲能案，若假設 2026/2027/2028 完工比率
10%/40%/50%計算，預計貢獻 7.5/30/37.5 億元，

說明： 
一、 公司治理評鑑成績高者顯示其公司治理較符合主管機關要求，適合 1 年以上之長期投資或存股標的，與投資評等(強力買進等)僅 1
年內之投資建議不同。 
二、 依臺灣證券交易所及證券櫃檯買賣中心委託證券暨期貨市場發展基金會辦理的公司治理評鑑，每年將受評公司區分為 「上市公司」 、
「上櫃公司」 兩組，由70 項指標(1.維護股東權益及平等對待股東 11 項，占 16%，2.強化董事會結構與運作 18 項，占 25%，3.提
升資訊透明度 7 項，占 10%，4.推動永續發展 34 項，占 49%)編製而成，各組依評鑑成績高低分別公布七級距之公司名單，台新
投顧係引用民國 114(2025)年度之評鑑結果資料。 
三、 轉登證基會警語：本評鑑系統使用之評鑑方法受有限制，評鑑工作善盡相當之注意義務以確認各評鑑指標之執行及資訊透明，惟評
鑑結果僅顯示該企業之公司治理在本評鑑指標下之運作狀況，不宜過度仰賴，使用者必須自行衡量資訊的價值，主辦單位及執行單
位不為使用者之資訊決策及結果負擔任何責任。

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 3 日

1 群益投顧 
市 場 流 傳 一 份 疑 似 台 達 電(2308 TT) 發 給 客 戶 的 通 知 指 出，特 定 批 次 電 源 供 應 器 存 在 潛
在 品 質 問 題 。 此 次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件 ， 並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計 ， 且 台 達 電 擁 有
不 同 電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決， 影 響 有 限。 台達電
為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商 ， 受 惠 於AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動 AC/DC 電 源 大 幅
成長外， CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC 電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成
長動能 ， 投 資 評 等 為 Buy ，目標價為 2,050 元。

研 究 員 ： 王 美 珍   
iris.wang@capital.com.tw

電 容 器 更 換 事 件 對 營 運 影 響 有 限：  
市場流傳一份疑似台達 電 發給客戶的通知指出，特定批次電源供應器存在
潛 在 品 質 問 題 。內 容 表 示 根 據 調 查 ， 受 影 響 PSU 所 使 用 的 部 分 電 容 器 ， 在
未 施 加 電 壓、長 時 間 存 放 後，可 能 出 現 漏 電 流 增 加 情 況，特 定 條 件 下 恐 進 一
步 造 成 電 容 器 電 壓 不 平 衡 及 過 熱，重 新 上 電 時 可 能 導 致 電 容 器 故 障。此 次 涉
及台達 電 新一代 VR 平台 18.3 kW PSU ， 相 關 問 題 於 試 產 階 段 發 現 ， 台 達電
隨 後 通 知 客 戶 並 進 行 電 容 器 更 換 。 
台達 電 表 示 不 評 論 市 場 傳 聞；若 相 關 情 況 確 認 屬 供 應 商 零 組 件 問 題，公 司 將
依 實 際 狀 況 更 換 供 應 商 ， 並 配 合 客 戶 需 求 進 行 後 續 處 理 。 
此次 問 題 主 要 來 自 供 應 商 零 組 件，並 非 台 達 電 產 品 設 計，且 台 達電 擁 有 不 同
電 容 器 供 應 商 及 備 料 ， 預 期 可 透 過 更 換 電 容 器 供 應 來 源 解 決 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ，
營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評 等 為Buy ， 目 標 價 為 2, 050 元。

相 關 個 股 獲 利 預 估 、 評 價 及 投 資 建 議

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 9 月 3 日

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We hosted an online conference call for NYPCB on 2 
September, after trading hours. Overall, we believe the price hike trend is 
largely on track with our estimates despite a short hiccup in 4Q26E (for 
more details, please refer to our earlier note, Escalating price hike in 
substrates). Looking ahead, we believe the trend towards enlarging body 
sizes and multiple core layers in ABF substrates and the supply-demand 
imbalance in BT substrates will be key growth drivers for NYPCB.

What’s the impact: Near-term update. We are comfortable with our 
revenue growth estimate of c.20% QoQ in 3Q26E, given ongoing price 
hikes and a rising utilisation rate at Kunshan (likely to reach 100% in 
4Q26E). We believe the yield rate at Kunshan still has room to improve, 
which could negatively impact the gross margin (GM) performance in 
3Q26E. That said, compared with July, we expect the GM to improve 6-7pp 
MoM in August given the rising yield rate, and remain confident of a c.30% 
GM in 3Q26E. We expect NYPCB to achieve higher price hikes than other 
substrate vendors, as it does not sign long-term agreements with end 
customers. However, NYPCB has stated that it currently has no intention to 
hike BT substrate prices further from 4Q26, as raw material price hikes 
have eased recently; as such, we have accounted for it in our model. 
Lastly, NYPCB mentioned that it has received new AI HDI orders from a 
US-based ODM, which will result in its PCB segment reaching c.30% QoQ 
revenue growth and operating profit from 4Q26E.

Long-term investment thesis. We believe a meaningful spec upgrade is 
also likely to occur in ABF substrates, as core layer counts will need to 
increase their rigidity to handle the increasing body size of substrates. This 
trend would digest more ABF capacity and relevant raw materials (eg, T-
glass) than just unit growth from 2028E. Also, we believe this would make 
the current upcycle last longer than previous ones. As for BT substrates, 
we believe ASP growth will accelerate again as two key players are 
gradually reducing their BT capacities. Thus, we maintain our BT ASP 
assumption for 2027E despite a lower-than-expected price hike in 4Q26E.

What we recommend: While we lower the 2026E EPS by 4% on a 
reduced BT price hike in 4Q26E, we keep the 2027-28E EPS largely 
unchanged. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and raise our 12-month TP to 
TWD2,460 (from TWD2,444), based on an unchanged target PER of 49x 
applied to the 1-year forward EPS. Downside risk: weaker-than-expected 
demand for switches.

How we differ: Our 2026-28E EPS are 10-41% above the Bloomberg 
consensus, likely due to our more bullish view on ABF/BT ASP hikes.

Company profile 
Nan Ya PCB originated as one of the business divisions of Nan Ya Plastics Corporation before 
transforming into a subsidiary in 1997, specialising in the manufacturing of printed circuit boards (PCBs) 
and integrated circuit (IC) substrates. The company’s primary end applications include networking 
(c.50%), PCs (c.20%), consumer electronics (c.10%), automotive (c.10%) and other sectors. Its 
manufacturing facilities are located in Taiwan and China.

Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
Nan Ya PCB's board of directors (BoD) is responsible for overseeing the company's operations
and ensuring adherence to corporate governance principles. The board comprises individuals
with diverse expertise, contributing to effective decision-making and strategic planning. The
company emphasizes the importance of corporate governance as a set of processes, customs,
policies, laws, and institutions affecting the way the corporation is directed, administered, or
controlled. Principal stakeholders include stockholders, management, and the board of directors,
among others.
Capital management 2
Nan Ya PCB maintains a prudent approach to capital management, ensuring financial stability
and the availability of resources necessary for operational and strategic initiatives. The
company's financial statements reflect a solid capital structure, supporting its growth and
sustainability objectives.
Related party &
transaction 1
The company adheres to strict policies regarding related party transactions to maintain
transparency and fairness. Such transactions are conducted under terms comparable to those
with independent third parties and are disclosed in financial reports to ensure compliance with
regulatory standards and corporate governance best practices. For example, the company's
consolidated financial statements provide details on transactions with related parties, ensuring
transparency and accountability.
S Supply chain
management 1
Nan Ya PCB is committed to sustainable supply chain management, emphasizing environmental
protection, procurement policies, and social responsibility. The company has established a
comprehensive procurement policy that integrates sustainability considerations, ensuring that
suppliers adhere to ethical standards and environmental regulations. This commitment is part of
the company's broader sustainable responsibility policy, which includes areas such as corporate
governance, environmental protection, and social welfare.
E Data security 1
While specific details on Nan Ya PCB's data security measures are not extensively disclosed in
the available sources, the company recognizes the importance of information security in its
operations. As part of its corporate governance framework, Nan Ya PCB is likely to implement
standard data protection practices to safeguard sensitive information and ensure compliance
with relevant regulations. Stakeholders are encouraged to consult the company's official
communications or contact the company directly for detailed information on data security
policies.

CHPT
Valuation
We value CHPT using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and the structural shift towards high-end
testing. Our target price of NT$4,350 is derived from applying 35x to 2027E EPS, implying the company's average forward PE
since 2024 +1 SD, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the 2nm/3nm testing segment,
rising contribution from US AI GPU & AI ASIC projects, and the unique competitive advantage of its "All-in-House" vertical

integration model. With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play
(rather than a consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant system rates the CHPT stock as High Risk for its share price volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price, and to our earnings forecasts, include: 1) margin

compression from lower-than-expected utilization rates; 2) execution delays in concentrated US AI GPU and ASIC programs;
3) intensifying competition in testing boards from global peers; and 4) structural gross margin dilution from a shift towards
high-end burn-in boards.

Analysts’ compensation is determined by Citi Research management and Citigroup’s senior management and is based upon
activities and services intended to benefit the investor clients of Citigroup Global Markets Inc. and its affiliates (the “Firm”).
Compensation is not linked to specific transactions or recommendations. Like all Firm employees, analysts receive
compensation that is impacted by overall Firm profitability which includes investment banking, sales and trading, and

principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to
participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 6

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

ab 6 September 2026
Global Research
Largan Precision
Strong CPO ramp priced ahead of qualification
The re-rating has outpaced qualification progress; downgrade from Buy to Sell
Largan shares have risen 242% since April on optimism around its CPO FAU progress.
While we stay constructive on Largan's CPO prospects, we believe the market is pricing
in confidence ahead of still-early qualification progress, particularly given a crowded
competitive field. Largan only starts its sample line late Q3, integrating its Prism Micro

Lens Array and Fiber Array with AOET's V-groove and Corning's fiber. We expect  several
quarters to improve yields, gain integrator acceptance (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and
convert its initial production-oriented FA program into volume while competing against
incumbent TFC and vertically integrated rivals incl. Coherent. We downgrade our rating
from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-
18x range well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 PT based on 15x smartphone lens

+ 50x CPO base case for 40% share  (upper end of optical peers  at 14-72x).
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low) and
1 FA design in an FAU. A risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028

and also competes with incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and
other FAU vendors like Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not
requiring Largan's initial V-groove approach. Largan's push toward a complete solution
may also conflict with integration partners.
Core smartphone lens tracking behind amid memory headwinds
We also see risk on near-term results with Q326 sales tracking light. Aug sales were NT
$5bn, +3% MoM, -16% YoY, taking QTD revenue to NT$10.1bn, 53% of UBSe/Street

Q326. Sept would need to grow +73% MoM to meet UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ vs.
normal seasonal +8% MoM. We expect flattish builds at best for its flagship customer
(80% of sales) needing to raise price to pass on memory costs +US$200-300 YoY albeit
some support from high-end introducing variable-aperture lens, while foldable lens
specs remain similar. Factoring muted smartphone growth but healthy margins, we view
a mid-teens multiple appropriate for this business, in line with its historical range.

Stock prices in our upside case ahead of traction; risk-reward skews negative
We maintain basic '26E/27E EPS at NT$190/NT$214 and NT$5,000 SOTP PT based on 1)
15x smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730
(40% share, 100k switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of
peers due to its premium ~40-45% OpM. We view risk/reward skewed negative with
downside to NT$2,500 on limited CPO success and softer smartphones, base case NT

$5,000 for CPO traction, and upside 22% if the stock is bid to our upside case NT$9,000
based on top-end 70x P/E on CPO earnings at 60% share of a higher US$1.58bn TAM.

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 2
Largan Precision UBS Research
Thesis Map     UBS                    Research     THESIS MAP a guide to our thinking and what´s where in this report
Q: Could Largan's CPO products contribute meaningful revenue in a few years?
We stay constructive but the company still has hurdles to clear in a crowded space. Its scale and track
record in high-end smartphone lens gives it an opportunity supplying key components of the CPO

FAU assembly, traditional strength in precision lens micro assemblies, alignment accuracy, in-house
tooling and automation that could give it a differentiated solution. Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan
suggests the company is still early in qualification, with its sample production line for customer
evaluation readied in late September and still requiring work with a system integrator and
qualification with the fabless customer and ODMs.  We still see a year to move from sampling to

design-in but if successful timed to meet expected market acceleration in 2028-29.
Q: Will there be further technology upgrades driving the lens cycle in the coming years?
We expect spec upgrades for Largan's core lens business to come in H226 from variable aperture
shipments for the iPhone 18 (which Genius does not currently participate though may enter over
time, as seen with periscope lenses). Key offset though is weaker sales with Q3 tracking light and

suggesting a softer build due to memory headwinds. Further upside could come from broader
adoption of 48MP modules in future iPhone models. Looking longer term, Apple is reportedly
exploring 200MP periscope lens and adjacent opportunities to penetrate physical AI categories with
precision lens including robotics, autonomous systems and AR/VR devices.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell as we view the market expectations as now
pricing in a bullish scenario for strong market traction with the company still at an early stage of

qualification, still needing to improve its process and yields, and competing in a crowded space also
against an existing incumbent. We expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain
acceptance from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI GPU
fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and other rivals. We downgrade
Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x

range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens +
50x CPO base case for40% share  (upper-end of optical peer range of 14-72x).
Largan has demonstrated its samples with partners at Computex and Semicon Taiwan and cited its
goals to ramp CPO as its next opportunity. The company is still early in qualifications with a sample
automated line in the coming months followed by design-in engagement with system integrators,

ODMs and the fabless customer to target design-in on 3rd gen CPO solutions in 2028. For
smartphone lens, its top customer had a strong iPhone 17 cycle although acknowledged it would
have to raise price to pass on higher memory costs which could dampen growth. Sales QTD are
tracking slightly light as Largan also competes with 2nd source Genius. Our supply chain checks and
management commentary points to introduction of higher value variable aperture lens in H226.

Largan is currently trading above the high end of its historical range at 39x/35x our 2026E/27E EPS vs.
a long-term range of 13-18x forward consensus earning, pricing in success penetrating the CPO
component market ahead of the earnings stream which would only start late in 2027 into 2028. We
note optical component peers trade at 14-72x with an average of ~38x 2027E P/E (Genius at 25x
2027 P/E). We maintain our SOTP valuation with our price target at NT$5000 based on 1) 15x

smartphone lens (NT$3,270) + 2) 50x CPO base case EPS of NT$35 for NT$1,730 (40% share, 100k
switches/7.2mn FAUs, US$150/FAU, US$1.1bn TAM), high-end of peers due to its premium ~40-
45% OpM. We view the stock at NT$7,400 now pricing in CPO traction on a 50x multiple of our
upside NT$76 case based on 60% share of an upside US$1.6bn opportunity.

### Page 3

Largan Precision 6 September 2026 ab 3
Largan Precision UBS Research
commentary
Largan shares have risen 242% since 1 April on optimism around its CPO fiber array unit
(FAU) progress. While we stay constructive post our feedback at Semicon Taiwan on
Largan's ability to develop CPO solutions, we now believe the market is pricing in a level
of confidence that has run well ahead of qualification progress which is still early and
competing in a crowded field. Largan will only start its sample line in late Q3 integrating

its Prism Micro Lens Array and Fiber Array, AOET's V-groove and Corning's glass fiber.
From there, we expect several quarters to improve current low yields, gain acceptance
from solution integrators (Corning, Senko, AuthenX) and secure design-ins by the AI
GPU fabless company and its ODM partners against incumbent TFC and rivals including
Coherent with own lens/FAU. The company did note on its Q226 call that it had secured
one mass production commitment for its fiber array to an FAU maker that could ramp by

H227 although early to conclude the magnitude of this engagement or end customer
design-in, with the other customers still at proof-of-concept phase.
We downgrade our rating on Largan from Buy to Sell with CPO still at an early stage and
the stock now 35x 2027 vs. LT 13-18x range and well above our sum-of-the-parts NT
$5,000 price target based on 15x smartphone lens + 50x CPO base case for 40% share
(upper-end of optical component peers trading at a  range of 14-72x).

Source: UBS estimates
Still a range of outcomes for CPO solution traction
Largan's approach pairs V-groove with PMLA to shape the optical light from the PIC into
the fiber at high accuracy with automated assembly. Feedback is positive on Largan's
precision and track record for volume production/yield improvement (now still low). A
risk is Largan targeting the Gen 3 optics should take until 2028 and also competes with

incumbent TFC which is already designed-in for 2026-27 and other FAU vendors like
Coherent that can vertically integrate or use meta lens not requiring Largan's initial V-
groove approach. Largan's business model may also conflict with integration partners as
it also attempts to build toward a complete solution.
We estimate Largan's Total Addressable Market in a downside case is US$270mn if it
only supplies sub-components into a market that continues to push out CPO due to

reliability concerns relative to pluggables and NPO/XPO options, US$1.08bn in a base
case with 100k switches and 72 FAUs per switch at US$150 ASP , and US$1.6bn in an
upside case with 125k switches, 72 FAUs per switch and US$175 ASP .  For Largan, we
estimate downside case 10% share yields NT$810mn sales and NT$2.20 EPS, base case
40% share, NT$13.0bn sales and NT$35 EPS, and upside case 60% share, NT$28.4bn
sales and NT$76 EPS.
Although we are optimistic on the market opportunity scaling up and Largan's

competencies and progress developing these solutions, Largan's traction is not yet
certain and it will compete in a crowded landscape. We believe Himax is already
supplying wafer level optics for the collimator function with FOCI (3363.TWO) and TFC
(300394.SZ) already has demonstrated capability and design-in on the FAU and lens
assembly. Others that could vertically integrate include Coherent, Senko, Corning and
Sumitomo, and we also note Genius sending samples and Sunny Optical's intention to

develop for this market.

Our feedback at Semicon Taiwan indicates Coherent can make the FAU and lens by itself
through capabilities merging with II-VI and demonstrated all in-house components at
the booth except for the metal transceiver casing.

Smartphone lens business tracking behind, should have a mild 2026-27
Largan reported August sales at NT$5bn,   is below seasonal 63-67% at only 53% of
Q326 UBSe/Street +36-38% QoQ and down 16% YoY, suggesting muted momentum
into its flagship customer (~80% of sales). July EPS was NT$13.94, 27% of UBSe Q3 NT
$51.52 suggesting in-line GM/OpM at ~50%/39% but the lighter sales are more of a

concern.
UBS expects flattish builds at best and a mild spec upgrade with one model variable
aperture but foldable at similar lens spec and higher memory cost (+US$200-300 YoY).
We still see mild growth and mid-teens multiple acceptable for the smartphone
business, similar to its long-term average.

UPSIDE (NT$9,000): Our upside valuation of NT$3,655 is based on  its average P/E of 16x

BASE (NT$5,000):  Our price target for its smartphone business is NT$3,270, with a 15x
2027 P/E applied on its base case EPS of NT$214 slightly below the long-term average of

its historical P/E band to reflect the macro uncertainty. Paired with our base scenario of its
CPO business at NT$35 EPS and 50x P/E, we arrive at an implied valuation of NT$1,730,
and Largan's aggregate PT  at NT$5,000.
DOWNSIDE (NT$2,500):   Downside valuation at NT$2,392, based on low-end of its
range 13x 2027 P/E. We take into consideration slower adoption of FAUs at its downside
EPS of NT$2 and the same 50x P/E to  an implied valuation of NT$108 for

CPO contribution, and Largan's total share price at NT$2,500.

Forecast returns
Forecast price appreciation -32.4%

Forecast stock return -31.4%
Market return assumption 6.3%
Forecast excess return -37.6%
Company Description
Founded in 1987, Largan Precision (Largan) is one of the world's leading manufacturers of
lenses used in cameras and IT products. It specialises in plastic/hybrid lenses and, to a lesser
extent, glass lenses and modules. In 2022, 20MP+ camera lenses were 10-20% of Largan's

shipment mix, 10MP lenses were 50-60%, 8MP lenses were 0-10% and other lenses were
30-40%.
Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target is based on SOTP valuation on the CPO opportunity and traditional lens
business.
We think Largan's downside risks include: 1) ongoing pricing pressure from smartphone OEM
clients and increasingly intense competition among lens suppliers; and 2) a slower-than-
expected smartphone demand recovery in China, and 3) CPO commercial success below the

market's elevated expectations.
We believe the key upside risk is a faster-than-expected CPO qualification process at Largan.
In addition, if the company demonstrates exceptionally efficient and highly accurate
automation capabilities, it could support greater market share gains within the industry than
we currently anticipate.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

WinWay Technology
Valuation
We value WinWay using a PE-based approach that factors in earnings momentum and likely expansion of its addressable
market. Our target price of NT$11,500 is derived from applying c.41x to 2H27E-1H28E EPS, higher than the upper end of its
long-term PE range, which we believe is justified given the company's entrenched position in the high-end AI GPU testing
supply chain, rising contribution from coaxial socket and SLT solutions, and the increasing value of test content per module.

With these structural drivers in place, and as we believe the market is starting to value it as an AI/HPC play (rather than a
consumer electronics stock), we expect the multiple to hold at a premium relative to its historical averages.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the WinWay stock High Risk based on its historical volatility.
Key downside risks that could impede the stock from reaching our target price and our earnings forecasts include: 1) client and

sector concentration – specifically any delays in US AI GPU product roadmap could impact the business outlook for WinWay; 2)
intensifying competition in coaxial and hypersockets from global peers; 3) faster-than-expected testing algorithm
optimization, which could shorten testing durations and reduce the anticipated hardware demand multiplier; and 4) delay of
MEMS Phase 2 mass production.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

M
Update
Advantech  | Asia Pacific
August Sales -8% MoM/+88%
YoY
In this report, we focus on Advantech's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• August sales were NT$10,419mn (-8% MoM/+88% YoY),
• QTD revenue reached 77% of MSe, at NT$28,185mn (+8% QoQ/+59% YoY),
and 76% of consensus, at NT$28,371mn (+9% QoQ/+60% YoY).
• By region, N.A (+106%) outperformed, while other markets, including Europe
(+52%), China (+67%), Taiwan (+96%), Japan (+73%), Korea (+79%)  and

emerging markets (+75%),  also performed strongly.
• By segment, Intelligent Systems was +93% YoY,   IoT Automation was +36%
YoY, while  Intelligent Service grew single-digit% YoY. The Embedded Sector
grew 61% YoY, of which Embedded IoT grew 83% YoY, while Applied
Computing grew 37% YoY.
Our view:
• We think its 3Q26 revenue is tracking ahead of market expectations and
more likely to approach 20-25% QoQ growth.
• Management attributed the strength in August to demand from sectors

including semi, AI datacenter, energy infrastructure, and factory automation.
• B/B ratio in August was stronger than that in July, continuing to point to a
strong revenue outlook in the coming quarters.
• We remain constructive on Advantech, as it is well positioned to benefit
from accelerating IoT market growth amid edge AI proliferation.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Advantech (2395.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We view our target multiple as justified by our 30% earnings CAGR
forecast for 2025-28. It implies a PEG of 1.0, vs. the stock's average one-year forward P/E of
25x in 2015-22 and the earnings CAGR of 8% during that period, with a PEG of 3.5.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected macroeconomic conditions
n More top- and bottom-line growth from the platform and applications

Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro environment
n Slower progress in edge AI implementation

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Bull/Bear: Aspeed Technology (527 4.TWO)

### Page 6

Aspeed Technology (5274.TWO)
09 September 2026 Citi Research
6
Aspeed Technology
Company description
Established in 2004 and listed in 2013, Aspeed is a leading IC design
company based in Taiwan focusing on baseboard management controller
(BMC) solutions. Aspeed started the business by targeting datacenter
servers/white-box markets and has been working closely with server ODMs.
Following the acquisition of Emulex Pilot from AVGO/Broadcom in 2016,

Aspeed successfully expanded its product lines, IP pool as well as customer
base and became the largest BMC supplier with c.70% of global market share.
Other than BMC, Aspeed is also broadening its product portfolio by including
bridge IC (BIC; or mini-BMC), platform firmware resilience (PFR) solutions and
input/output (I/O) expander, aiming to maximize the dollar content it could
generate per server motherboard.
Investment strategy
We rate Aspeed shares a Buy. As a leading BMC supplier with c.70%+ global

market share, it has been riding the strong momentum underpinned by a
recovery of general-purpose servers and upticks in AI servers. We expect the
growth to continue. Apart from being in a dominant position for the supply of
upcoming GB200 server BMCs with higher dollar content and better capex
efficiency, new product launches including AST2700, PFR, I/O expander and
Cupola 360, as well as steady growth in general-purpose servers should also
act as key drivers for the company.

Valuation
We value Aspeed at NT$25,000 based on a historical mid-cycle multiple
(since 2020) of 50x 2028E EPS, as we believe it fairly reflects the company’s
earnings performance and industry dynamics, secular growth trends, and
Aspeed’s strong supply chain position, as well as robust earnings growth
amid margin expansions.
Risks
Our quant rating system rates the Aspeed stock High Risk based on its
volatility over the past year, which we think is unwarranted given the

company's dominance and outlook in the BMC sector. Key downside risks to
our earnings forecasts and target price include: 1) weaker-than-expected
growth in servers; 2) slower-than-expected CPU platform migration; 3) peer
competition; and 4) limited progress in product diversification.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Wistron

Reiterate Buy: Aug sales +49% MoM, aided 
by solid AI momentum; lift PO to NT$265

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 265.00 TWD | Price: 189.00 TWD

Aug’26 sales +49% MoM, mainly driven by AI 
Wistron’s Aug’26 sales of NT$460bn grew 49% MoM (+166% YoY), with subsidiary 
Wiwynn’s Aug sales of NT$144bn grew 23% MoM (+50% YoY). Wistron’s strong Aug 
sales were mainly driven by enterprise AI server projects, with overall AI server rack 
shipments roughly doubling MoM. Given the strong Aug sales, we lift our 3Q26 sales 
forecast by 11%. We expect Sep sales to decline 20% MoM to NT$370bn from the high 
Aug base. Yet, Sep sales should remain above July’s NT$308bn as we stay positive on AI 
order momentum into following quarters, driven by Nvidia’s new Vera Rubin generation.  
PC remains soft; Aug shipments down 13% MoM 
Wistron’s Aug NB shipments were 1.4mn (-13% MoM, -36% YoY). We view it as in line 
with supply chain’s commentary on weaker demand in 2H26 amid continued component 
cost inflation issue. This also echoes Wistron management’s cautious tone that PC 
shipment should decline 10-20% HoH in 2H26 following earlier order pull-in 1H26. We 
now expect 3Q26 NB shipment to fall 18% QoQ, implying 1.5mn units of NB shipments 
in Sep (+8% MoM, -31% YoY) with potential quarter-end pull-in demand. 
Lift PO to NT$265; reiterate Buy on solid AI momentum 
We lift our 2026-28E EPS by 4-6% to reflect higher sales, mainly driven by AI (with 
diversifying clients/projects), better operating leverage, and actual 2Q26 data. We raise 
our PO to NT$265 from NT$238, with our valuation method unchanged at 15x 2H26-
1H27E EPS. We reiterate Buy on Wistron’s solid multi-year AI server outlook. We expect 
a positive near-term market reaction to the strong Aug sales.

Key Metrics

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wistron is one of the top-5 ODMs globally. Server/storage 
and PC business accounted for 73% and 24% of sales in 
2025, respectively. Key server/storage customers include 
Dell, Lenovo, HPE, Cisco, Nvidia, AMD, Intel. Key NB 
customers include Dell, Lenovo, Acer. Wistron was spun off 
from Acer (2353 TT) in 2000 and was incorporated in May 
2001. It went publicly listed on the Taiwan stock exchange 
in Aug 2003. It is headquartered in Taipei, Taiwan.

Investment Rationale 
We assign a Buy rating to Wistron in view of improving 
sales/profit mix, solid outlook from Wiwynn, high exposure 
to server business after exiting from low-margin iPhone 
assembly business, and improving OPM. We believe 
Wistron will benefit from Dell's strong demand in Nvidia AI 
GPU server rack as the key supplier.

### Page 7

Price objective basis & risk 
Wistron (WICOF) 
Our PO of NT$265 is based on 15x 2H26-1H27E EPS. We use 15x valuation multiple 
(around 1.5SD above historical P/E average since 2022) in view of high server mix, a key 
position in AI server supply chain with exposure both to component/rack business, and 
ongoing OPM improvement.

Upside risks to our PO are (1) stronger server market demand, especially for AI, (2) 
stronger PC demand, (3) share/order win in AI server business, and (4) favorable FX 
trend.

Downside risks to our PO are (1) softer AI server demand due to AI GPU short supply or 
supply chain hiccup, (2) weaker PC market demand amid inflation/macro uncertainties, 
(3) GM pressure due to higher labor/logistics cost, (4) more intensified competition in 
NB and server business, and (5) unfavorable FX movement (USD/RMB and/or USD/NTD) 
to weigh on GM and/or non-operating items.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 - Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst ’s view of the potential price 
appreciation (depreciation).

Greater China Technology Hardware

Industry View In-Line
Our CY26 full-year forecast is now at 80-85K racks: This year is a strong one for
downstream rack assembly. We expect over 100% y/y growth in rack shipments, vs.

~26.5k last year.
Quanta (2382.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$424.0B (+16% m/m, +177% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth mainly to higher GB200/300 rack shipments at 2.5-
2.55K racks (+22% m/m), despite offset slightly by lower NB shipments at 2.6M
units (-7% m/m, -37% y/y). Combining July and August GB200/300 rack shipments,
Quanta has already shipped ~4.6K racks, which would imply upside to our current
3Q forecast of ~6.4K racks. We think there is likely also upside to consensus

forecast, since combined July+August revenue reached ~NT$790B, which is 81% of
MSe and 71% of consensus 3Q revenue estimates. This also implies that there could
be upside to our full-year estimate of ~18.7K racks.
Wistron (3231.TW, OW): August revenue was  NT$460.1B (+49% m/m, +166% y/y).
We attribute the m/m growth to: 1) materially higher GB200/300 server computing
tray shipments  (2.3-2.4K rack equivalents, by our estimates, up approximately 100%

m/m), and higher Wiwynn revenue at ~NT$144.3B (+23% m/m, +50% y/y), while
offset by lower NB shipments at 1.4M units (-13% m/m, -36% y/y), lower DT
shipments at 500K (-29% m/m, -38% y/y) and lower monitor shipments at 750K (-
17% m/m, -6% y/y). Combined July + August revenue reached ~NT$768.3B, which is
74% of MSe and 75% of consensus 3Q revenue estimate, implying upside to 3Q. This
also implies that there could be upside to our full-year forecast of ~17.9K racks.

Hon Hai (2317.TW, OW): August revenue was NT$921.8B (-3% m/m, +52% y/y), with
server-related revenue seeing the strongest m/m growth, followed by components
and other products, while computing products and consumer segment, which mainly
includes iPhone assembly, declined m/m. We model HH's GB rack shipments at 2.6K
racks (+6% m/m). Our full-year forecast is unchanged, at ~31.7K racks.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.
Key Takeaways
We estimate GB200/300 rack output in August at ~8.4K (+27% m/m), within
which:
Quanta shipped 2.5-2.55K  GB200/GB300 racks.
Wistron shipped 2.3-2.4K GB200/GB300 racks.
Hon Hai shipped ~2.6K GB200/300 racks.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Hon Hai Precision (2317.TW)
Base case scenario value, derived from a residual income valuation model. Our key assump-
tions include a cost of equity of 8.5%, a medium-term growth rate of 13%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected iPhone sell-through
n Faster progress in AI server business
n Faster EV business development progress
n Any new M&A activity that could improve sentiment

Risks to Downside
n Lower iPhone sell-through
n Smaller profit contribution from AI business
n Slower EV business development progress

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 2

iPhone 18 Pro and Pro Max: (1) Cooling: the new iPhone is embedded with a more 
powerful SoC (A20 Pro) and Apple Intelligence, making thermal management essential 
to the device. The next generation of Vapor Chamber is used in the new iPhones, which 
we see as positive for the cooling suppliers. Read more: AVC, AAC; (2) Camera: variable 
aperture is adopted in rear cameras, in which lens aperture changes based on the focal 
length, allowing easier user control of DoF (depth of field), taking pictures in low lighting, 
or having a more natural broken gradient background when in portrait mode. Read 
more: Largan. Selling price is higher by US$100 (or high single-digit %) compared to 
iPhone 17 Pro series, with the same delivery time.  
Apple Watch and Airpods: Airpods 5 feature active noise cancellation, and are 
designed to allow more natural interaction (e.g. transparency mode, better conversation 
awareness). The wireless charging case models are US$30 cheaper compared to Airpods 
4. Apple Watch 12 and Ultra 4 are priced the same as the previous generation with 
better specifications: health sensing system with the most accurate heart rate sensing in 
a wearable, Siri recap, audio intelligence, etc.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Presenters: Wendell Huang, SVP & CFO  
Bottom Line: We have three key takeaways: (1) TSMC continues to view AI as a 
multi-year megatrend that remains at a very early stage, with lower compute costs 
expected to accelerate AI adoption and ultimately drive greater demand for 
advanced silicon; (2) execution in Arizona continues to progress well, with Phase 1 
achieving yields comparable to Taiwan and already generating profits, while TSMC 
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside 
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona; and (3) despite dilution from the N2 ramp 
and overseas expansion, management remains comfortable achieving its long-term 
gross margin target of 56% and above, supported by disciplined capacity planning, 
high utilization, productivity improvements, cost reductions, and pricing that reflects 
TSMC’s value. 
Key Takeaways

AI remains in the early stages of a multi-year megatrend: TSMC continues to n
see increasing adoption of AI. Management also highlighted the shift from 
generative AI toward agentic AI, which should broaden compute demand 
beyond accelerators to CPUs. TSMC remains confident in the durability of the AI 
cycle based on discussions not only with its direct customers but also with its 
customers’ customers. Recent visits of US CSPs further reinforced management’s 
confidence, as cloud service providers appeared well prepared in areas including 
power and broader infrastructure deployment. 
Lower compute costs should drive greater consumption and silicon demand: n
Management believes open-source models and continued improvements in 
computing efficiency will reduce token costs, leading to greater AI 
consumption and broader adoption, supporting incremental demand for 
advanced silicon. TSMC has already incorporated this dynamic into its capacity 
planning and continues to expand aggressively to support what it views as

### Page 2

structural, multi-year demand growth.
Disciplined demand validation remains critical amid aggressive expansion: n
TSMC uses a combination of top-down and bottom-up analysis to validate demand.
Its top-down assessment considers macroeconomic conditions, semiconductor
demand, fabless/system companies, the foundry industry, etc, while its bottom-up
approach evaluates demand across different segments and incorporates feedback
from customers and customers’ customers. Management acknowledged that

semiconductors will remain cyclical, but believes correctly identifying the underlying
megatrend is more important than attempting to forecast every short-term
fluctuation.
Arizona execution is demonstrating that TSMC’s manufacturing model can n
travel: TSMC reiterated that overseas expansion is based on customer demand for
geographic flexibility and an appropriate level of government support. Arizona
Phase 1, using N4 technology, is in operation, with yields comparable to Taiwan,

satisfactory quality and reliability, and positive profitability. Management views
this as an important milestone because it demonstrates internally and to customers
that TSMC can produce wafers in the US with the same quality as in Taiwan.
Equipment move-in for Phase 2, which will use N3 technology, is expected to begin
soon; construction of Phase 3 has started; and preparation work for Phase 4 and the
first US advanced-packaging facility is underway.

Arizona has substantial room for further expansion: In addition to its original n
Arizona development, TSMC has acquired another parcel of land capable of
accommodating an additional five to six fabs, supporting its recently
announced incremental US$100bn investment. Based on its current plan, TSMC
expects approximately 30% of its N2-and-beyond capacity to be located outside
Taiwan in five years, primarily in Arizona. However, the most advanced technologies

will continue to ramp first in Taiwan because the early-stage technology transfer
requires close collaboration and frequent movement of engineers among R&D and
manufacturing sites. Overseas deployment will generally follow after the technology
has stabilized in Taiwan.
Customers increasingly value and are willing to pay for geographic flexibility: n
Management noted that customers initially requested only a modest amount of
geographically diversified supply, but their requirements have increased as TSMC has

demonstrated successful execution in Arizona. TSMC believes this geographic
flexibility represents incremental value and intends to reflect that value in its pricing,
with customers increasingly willing to recognize the associated premium.
Technology, manufacturing excellence, capacity, and trust underpin TSMC’s n
competitive moat: Management believes customers select a foundry partner based
on access to the best technology, the ability to ramp rapidly into high-volume

manufacturing with attractive economics, sufficient capacity, and confidence that
the foundry will not compete with its customers. TSMC is confident in maintaining
this lead by continuing to advance its roadmap. Management also highlighted its
record of rapidly ramping new technologies, the scale of its wafer and
advanced-packaging investments, and its consistent delivery against customer
10 September 2026   2
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM

e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 3

commitments as key elements of customer trust that cannot be replicated through
CapEx or engineering recruitment alone.
Pricing remains strategic rather than opportunistic: TSMC does not intend to raise n
prices simply because near-term utilization is high, nor reduce prices simply because
utilization temporarily weakens. Instead, the company’s approach is to price
sustainably and consistently based on the value it provides, while ensuring that
customers can remain successful over the long term. Management noted that

TSMC’s structural profitability has continued to improve, with its long-term gross
margin target increasing from 50% five years ago to 53% and now 56% and above.
Leading-edge wafer and advanced-packaging capacity remain tight: n
Management continues to build capacity wherever possible to meet customer
demand. N3 demand remains particularly strong: unlike prior technology cycles,
when demand for a node typically began declining two to three years after its

introduction, TSMC is adding further N3 capacity and still does not expect supply
to be sufficient. The company is also expanding advanced-packaging capacity to
address strong accelerator demand. Outside TSMC, management identified power
and memory as potential bottlenecks cited by other parts of the AI supply chain,
while emphasizing that foundry capacity itself remains difficult to expand because
fabs require two to three years to build and another one to two years to ramp.

TSMC’s disclosed AI revenue understates its broader exposure: Management n
reiterated that its disclosed AI revenue definition is relatively narrow and primarily
captures GPUs, custom AI accelerators and memory base die. It excludes CPUs and
networking products because TSMC cannot reliably determine whether each chip is
ultimately deployed in an AI system. As agentic AI drives incremental demand across
x86, Arm, and RISC-V CPUs, as well as networking and other supporting silicon,

TSMC’s total AI-related exposure is therefore broader than its reported
AI-accelerator revenue. The company intends to retain its existing definition until it
can more accurately distinguish the end use of these products.
No visible barrier to continued transistor scaling: TSMC is currently ramping N2, n
with A14 expected approximately two years later, while development of the
subsequent node is already underway. Management does not currently see a

technical issue preventing delivery of the next node or continued advancement
beyond the existing roadmap. TSMC views close collaboration with customers as a
key foundry advantage, allowing the company to develop each technology against
specific customer performance, power, density, and economic requirements.
Cross-node capacity optimization is an underappreciated advantage: TSMC n
highlighted its ability to generate additional output through productivity

improvements and to convert tools and capacity across technology nodes. For
example, the company can use portions of its N5 capacity to support N3 demand
and can also optimize capacity across less-adjacent nodes where technically
feasible. Management views this cross-node flexibility as a distinctive element of
TSMC’s manufacturing excellence, allowing the company to respond more
quickly to demand changes while reducing the risk of capacity obsolescence.

Advanced packaging is strategic primarily because it enables wafer growth: n
10 September 2026   3
Goldman Sachs TSMC (2330.TW)For the exclusive use of KEVINLU@LENOVO.COM
e92c7a75ab8b4efbba794e6b187208c8

### Page 4

CoWoS capacity remains tight, and TSMC already started outsourcing selected 
packaging processes to OSAT partners to support additional growth. Management 
emphasized that advanced packaging is intended primarily to ensure and enable 
wafer demand rather than serve as a standalone profit pool. TSMC is therefore 
developing multiple packaging architectures, including panel-level packaging, etc, 
while remaining open to customers using alternative packaging providers, as either 
approach ultimately supports demand for TSMC wafers. 
Advanced-packaging execution remains a key differentiator: TSMC noted that its n
current advanced-packaging capability supports approximately 5.5x reticle size, 
with a roadmap to reach approximately 14x reticle sizes within the next several 
years. While competing roadmaps may also appear attractive, management believes 
execution and the ability to deliver in high-volume manufacturing will ultimately 
determine competitive outcomes. TSMC does not believe competitors can use 
advanced-packaging wins to meaningfully displace its leading-edge wafer 
business, as foundry and packaging remain fundamentally different businesses. 
A unified TSMC culture supports overseas manufacturing execution: TSMC’s n
objective is not to create separate Taiwan and US cultures, but to establish a 
consistent TSMC culture centered on integrity, commitment, innovation, and 
customer trust (ICIC). Locally hired Arizona employees spend six to eighteen months 
training and working in Taiwan, while experienced Taiwan-based employees are 
assigned to Arizona to work alongside local teams. Over time, the number of Taiwan 
assignees should decline as locally trained employees assume greater responsibility. 
TSMC is applying this approach across its overseas manufacturing sites. 
Management remains comfortable with its 56%-and-above long-term gross n
margin target: TSMC reiterated its 6 major drivers of profitability: new-technology 
ramp, pricing, cost, product mix, utilization, and FX. The N2 ramp is expected to 
dilute 2026 GM by approximately 2-3ppts, but its profitability should improve over 
time toward the corporate average; management also noted that N2’s structural 
profitability is better than N3 at a comparable stage. Overseas expansion is 
expected to dilute GM by 2-3ppts during the early portion of the current five-year 
period, then widening to 3-ppts later as more overseas fabs begin production. Each 
new fab will initially create dilution before improving along its learning curve. Taking 
these puts and takes together, management remains very comfortable that 
disciplined capacity planning, healthy utilization, cost reduction, productivity 
gains, and pricing that reflects TSMC’s value will enable the company to deliver 
for shareholders and achieve its long-term gross margin target ‘of 56% and 
higher’ with focus on the ‘higher’ side. 
Valuation/Risks

TSMC (Buy): Our 12-month price target is NT$3,100, which is derived by applying a 
target P/E multiple of 22x to our 2027E EPS. Our 22x target P/E is benchmarked against 
1.0stdv above its 5-year trading average. For the ADR (TSM), we have a 12-month price 
target of US$620, based on a USD/TWD rate of 30.0 and an ADR premium of 20%. Key

downside risks to our views: (1) further deterioration in end-demand recovery

yields/execution resulting in worse-than-expected profitability; (5) stronger

higher-than-expected cost increase weighing on the margin outlook.

3014 聯陽(ITE)

報告日期： 2026/09/11 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 2H26 營運將優於各大研調機構對全球 NB 預估數  
由於 AI 需求強勁帶動記憶體供不應求，價格相應上漲數倍以上，
客戶擔憂記憶體價格持續上漲而提前下單拉貨，使聯陽營運在
1Q26 淡季不淡逆勢成長後， 2Q26 營收持續成長至 18.35 億元，
QOQ+5.85% ，YOY-4.57% ，因上游封裝廠漲價影響在 1H26 仍不
明顯的情況下， 2Q26 整體毛利率仍維持在 57.21% 的高檔水準，
稅後淨利 4.88 億元 ，QOQ+6.45% ，YoY+28.41% ， 單季稅後EPS 
2.94 元，優於預期，合計 1H26 稅後 EPS 5.7 元。

因記憶體價格與去年同期相比已大漲 6～7 倍 ， 直接影響消費者對
3C 產品的購買意願 ， 因此 ， 各大研調機構均預估2026 年全球 NB
出貨量年減 10～14% 左右 ， 但聯陽除了PC/NB 相關產品外， 亦有
其他產品出貨成長，使聯陽整體營運將大幅優於全球 PC/NB 的表
現， 3Q26 為營運高峰，預估營收為 18.76 億元， QOQ+2.2% ，
YOY+4.53% ，惟毛利率將開始逐漸反映封裝成本上升的影響，使
2H26 整體毛利率表現低於 1H26 ，因此，預估聯陽 3Q26 單季稅
後 EPS 2.76 元。

二.逐步與客戶討論調漲產品價格 ， 反映原物料成本上漲 
過去一年記憶體價格大漲，雖然目前漲幅趨緩，但仍處於高檔，
包括 DDR、Embedded DRAM 等，其中與聯陽最直接相關應用於
SoC 、影像擷取運算產品的 Embedded DRAM ，價格已由去年初
的 1.5 美元上漲至目前的 10 美元，加上晶圓代工、封裝測試均朝
調漲價格的趨勢走，因此，聯陽在不影響客戶產品售價競爭力的
情況下，將與客戶討論 2H26 調漲價格事宜，以反映原物料成本
上漲的壓力，期能將毛利率守穩在 50% 以上。

三. SoC 打入印度 Tier 1 摩托車廠， 2H26 開始出貨  
聯陽目前產品營收比重為 EC 佔營收比重 50%( 因 SoC 大幅成長 ，
1H26 下滑至 40～45%) 、HSL( 高速傳輸介面 )佔 30% 、Others 佔
20+% ，與 PC/NB 相關的營收佔 70～80% ，且聯陽 NB 的 EC 在
全球市佔率 40～45% ，雖然市佔率再大幅提升的空間有限，但聯
陽持績進行大量的產品 Design in ，確保提供給客戶的產品為最新
規格及最具競爭力的，也能同步帶動聯陽的營運優於全球 NB 的
成長性，以及營運長期的成長動能。

2 
過去聯陽 SoC 產品主要應用於大陸白牌家電的顯示面板，目前已
導入汽車領域，應用於抬頭顯示器、後座的扶手屏，此外，也打
入印度 Tier 1 的機車儀表板， 2H26 開始放量出貨，為 2026 年成
長最大的產品。聯陽除了持續發展既有的 Non-NB 產品外，在 AI
朝向邊緣 AI 發展的趨勢下，聯陽已著手開發 Arm-base 的工業電
腦產品及與安全監控相關的邊緣 AI， 進一步擴大Non-NB 產品線 。

四. 預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 10.56/10.94 元 
2026 年全球 NB 市場雖然受到 DRAM 價格大幅上漲的影響 ， 可能
呈現 YoY-10 ～-15% 的衰退，但因聯陽具有優異的設計能力，在
EC 跟 Super I/O 的市佔率全球第一，協助客戶進行產品客製化，
與競爭者的產品做出差異化，使營運維持較佳的韌性，優於產業
的表現，此外，聯陽也將在與客戶在能達到雙贏的基礎之上，適
度的反映原物料成本的上漲，維持毛利率表現。綜上，我們預估
聯陽 2026 年的營收 71.37 億元， YoY+2.73% ，毛利率 55.2% ，
稅後淨利 17.53 億， YoY+14.5% ，稅後 EPS 10.56 元； 2027 年
雖然記憶體價格將維持在高檔水準，但對 3C 產品的影響性將趨
緩，加上 SoC 產品持續放量出貨下，我們預估聯陽 2027 年營收
80.46 億元 ，YoY+12.74% ， 毛利率53.36% ，稅 後EPS 10.94 元。

五. 投資建議為買進，目標價 175 元 
由於聯陽透過產品附加價值的提升，供應 IC 的數量逐漸增加，拉
高單位 NB 營收獲利貢獻度，因此在景氣波動時也能帶動營運表
現優於全球 PC/NB 出貨量的成長性，因此，即便因發行員工限制
型股票將影響 2026 年稅後 EPS-1.2 元， 2027 年影響將降至 -0.7
元，但整體獲利仍能穩健成長，且聯陽具有穩定配息的股利政策，
每年盈餘配發率 >80% ，為長期投資良好標的，投資建議為買進，
目標價 175 元(16X2027 稅後 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
聯陽半導體 (3014) 成立 1996 年，為一專業的  Fabless IC 設計公
司，早期深耕 PC 及 NB 控制晶片的開發設計，其 Super I/O( 輸出
入晶片 )及 Keyboard and Embedded Controller 晶片技術已是全
球領導者，其中 I/O 產品全球市佔率已超過四成，主要客戶為廣
達、仁寶、英業達等。因 PC/NB 為成熟型產業，聯陽積極進行產
品多角化，預估未來除了受益原有產品 Super I/O 升級外，在電子
紙驅動 IC、影像截取、多媒體處理、數位電視接收晶片亦可望陸
續開花結果。

二.營運概況及展望  
1、預估 2026 年全球 NB 出貨 YoY-15.18% 
在新冠疫情過後，及通膨的影響，品牌廠持續進行庫存去化，使
全球 NB 需求在 2022 年及 2023 年急速下降，在進行約 6～8 個
季度的庫存去化後，需求開始回穩，雖然要回到疫情期間的年增
雙位數不易 ， 但需求大致走穩 ， 使2024 年全球出貨量回升到 1.95
億台，YoY+2.42% ，2025 年在 Windows 10 EOL 及疫情期間購買
的 NB 逐漸進入換機潮的帶動下 ， 全球NB 出貨量持續呈現小幅成
長，預估為 2.02 億台， YoY+3.13% ，預估 2026 年在 AI 訓練需求
強勁了 3 年後 ， 推論的需求將開始接棒成長 ， 有利AI PC 的導入，
帶動全球 NB 成長，不過因為 AI 強勁的需求，且進入推理的代理
人 AI 後，對這憶體需求大幅成長，帶動 2Q25 後記憶體需求大幅
成長，呈現明顯供不應求，帶動記憶體報價爆漲，在記憶體價格
漲勢未歇的情況下，將影響終端的消費性電子產品，因此，預估
2026 年全球 NB 出貨量將下滑至 1.71 億台， YoY-15.18% 。

3231 緯創資通(WISTRON)

報告日期 ：2026/09/11           研究員 ： 曾智楷(Kevin Tseng) 
壹、投 資 建 議

一、 2Q26 獲利優於預期，毛利率 5.66% 
緯創 2Q26 營收 8954 億，QoQ +5.8% ，YoY +62.4% ，獲利方面，
2Q26 營業毛利 506.6 億， QoQ +14.9% ，YoY +107.2% ，毛利率
5.66% ，QoQ +0.45ppts ，YoY +1.22ppts ，營業利益 334.5 億，
QoQ +14.8% ，YoY +210.1% ，營利率 3.74% ，QoQ +0.3ppts ，
YoY +1.78ppts ，業外淨損 23.63 億，稅後淨利 148 億， QoQ 
+54% ，YOY +128% ，單季稅後 EPS 4.66 元，創單季新高。

二、受惠 AI 需求續強，，挹注下半年營收動能  
公司公告 8 月營收 4600.65 億， MoM +49.3% ，YoY +166.5% ，
今年累計營收 2.51 兆， YoY +98.9% 。展望 2H26 ，公司表示 AI
需求依舊非常強勁，受惠於新客戶以及新產品線的加入，預期 AI
伺服器下半年出貨量及營收表現將優於上半年。通用型伺服器則
預期下半年出貨持平。針對第三季新舊平台交替，公司表示，新
一代 Vera Rubin 已經開始出貨，且 GB 系列下半年仍有客戶持續
採購，並未出現新舊產品迭代的空窗期。而筆電部分，公司則是
表示，由於上半年 PC 客戶提前拉貨影響，預估下半年筆電出貨
量將較上半年衰退 10~20% 。預估公司 3Q26 營收 1.16 兆， QoQ 
+29.8% ，YoY +104.7% ，毛利率 5.7% ，營利率 3.6% ，稅後淨利
163.4 億，單季稅後 EPS 5.14 元。

三、預估 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 18.02/22.70 元 
展望 2026 年，公司預估， 2026 年 AI 伺服器的出貨將呈現倍增，
除了既有主要 Enterprise 客戶需求成長之外，下半年將進一步加
入新的 Enterprise 客戶與新的 CSP 客戶。而就新產品進度而言，
NVIDIA 新一代 VR 已經開始量產， CPU 機櫃、 LPU 機櫃、交換
器及儲存設備也會在下半年陸續出貨。此外， AMD MI450 系列也
會在下半年開始出貨。另外，網通方面，交換器 (Switch) 已開始出
貨給客戶，維持今年出貨量成長 10 倍以上預期，將有助於公司產
品組合的持續優化。此外，公司表示，受惠於客戶結構走向多元
化，有助於公司整體利潤率的提升。預估公司 2026 年全年營收
4.07 兆， YoY +86.5% ，毛利率 5.5% ，營利率 3.5% ，稅後淨利
572.9 億，全年稅後 EPS18.02 元。而就明年來看，公司對 2027
年展望持樂觀態度，由於新一代產品放量主要會落在 2027 年，再
加上交換機出貨亦將持續增加，並將推出 800G 、1.6T 等新規格
投資評等：買進

產品，營收、獲利規模將進一步提升。預估公司 2027 年全年營收

四、投資建議為「買進」，目標價 270 元 
考量到美系 CSP 資本支出展望正向，公司 AI 產品需求維持強勁
成長動能，且隨著公司新客戶以及新產品線的加入，將有助於公
司整體利潤率的提升，對公司今、明年展望維持正向。目前股價
交易於 2027 年 EPS 約 8~9 倍本益比，評價並未明顯偏高，給予
買進投資建議，目標價為 270 元(12x 2027 EPS) 。

貳、公 司 概 況

一. 公司簡介  
緯創資通 (Wistron) 成立於 2001 年 ， 前身為宏碁電腦的研製服務部
門，現為全球領先的資通訊產業專業代工大廠 (ODM) ，為全球頂
尖品牌提供從設計、製造到售後服務的全方位解決方案。公司核
心產品線涵蓋筆記型電腦、桌上型電腦、伺服器、儲存設備、工
業電腦、網路通訊設備及行動裝置等。近年在人工智慧運算需求
激增的背景下，緯創已成功轉化為 AI 伺服器基板與模組供應鏈中
的關鍵推手，成為全球 AI 硬體基礎架構的核心供應商。在產能佈
局上，緯創於台灣新竹、林口及高雄設有研發中心與高階生產線；
中國大陸則以昆山、成都為製造重心。為強化供應鏈韌性，公司
近年大幅擴張越南及馬來西亞產能，並於墨西哥與捷克設有規模
龐大的工廠，以就近服務北美及歐洲市場。主要客戶方面， AI 運
算與伺服器領域包含 NVIDIA 、 AMD 、 Intel 及各大雲端服務商
(Microsoft 、 Meta 、 Google 、 Amazon) ； PC 與消費電子則涵蓋
Dell 、HP、Lenovo 及 Acer 。主要競爭對手包括鴻海 (Foxconn) 、
廣達 (Quanta) 、仁寶 (Compal) 、英業達 (Inventec) 及和碩
(Pegatron) 。

1. 四大 CSP 上修今年資本支出達 7,450 億美元  
在生成式 AI 商機快速擴張帶動下，全球雲端基礎設施投資進入新
一輪高峰。亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和微軟認為其在先前的鉅額投資已
帶動營收明顯成長 ， 同時預估未來AI 潛在商機仍持續擴大 。 其中 ，
亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 已陸續上調 2026 全年投資金額，且四
大雲端服務供應商 (CSP) 總資本支出將上升至 7,450 億美元新高
規模。亞馬遜、微軟、 Alphabet 與 Meta 2026 年第 1、2 季分別
為 1,306 億、1,701 億美元，同時亞馬遜、 Alphabet 和 Meta 再上
修 2026 全年資本支出金額， 2026 年資本支出預估將達 7,450 億
美元。而根據 TrendForce 最新產業研究，全球主要雲端服務供應
商(CSP) 為加速建置 AI 基礎設施，資本支出預估於 2026 年將年
增 98%，2027 年再成長 50% 至 1.3 兆美元左右 ， 年增率收斂的主
要原因，除了反映高基期因素之外，部分可歸咎為鉅額資本支出
對四大 CSP 財務衝擊日益擴大。然而，在 AI Inference 、Agentic 
AI、自研 ASIC 和新一代 AI 模型持續推升算力需求的情況下，預
期整體 CSP 投資金額將續創新高紀錄，產業成長有望從原先以
GPU 擴張為主，轉為包含 AI server 、液冷散熱、先進封裝、高速
互聯、電源和記憶體等基礎設施全面升級的態勢。

圖二、 2024-2027 年全球九大 CSP 資本支出總額預估 (單位 :十億美元 )

資料來源： TrendForce 、宏遠投顧整理

2. 2026 年全球 AI 伺服器出貨量預計年增 31% 
根據 TrendForce 最新 AI server 產業研究，近期超大型 CSP 、
Tier-2 資料中心業者對 NVIDIA GB/VR 等整櫃式 AI server 方案的
採購意願明顯提高，且 Google 、AWS 新一代 ASIC 平台於 2026
下半年陸續放量，加上中系業者擴大採用本地 AI 方案以滿足雲端
大型語言模型 (LLM) AI 需求，促使 TrendForce 上調 2026 年 AI

方案，自研 ASIC 布局預計至 2027 年將明顯加速，為該公司長期
降低 AI 基礎設施成本、提升推論效率的重要策略。

3324.TT 雙鴻 
 水冷板重回 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 供應鏈

[財務資訊更新]

公司簡介 
雙鴻成立於 1998 年 8 月，主要生產散熱器、散熱片、散熱模組、散熱導管、風扇等，
應用於筆記型電腦 、 桌上型電腦 、 伺服器 、 投影機等領域 ， 主要終端客戶為Apple(AAPL 
US)、Dell(DELL US)、Samsung(005930 KS)、HP(HPQ US)、 宏碁(2353)、 華碩(2357)
等。 
投資評等/目標價 重點摘要 
買進 1,620 
【 前 次 投 資 建 議 】區 間 操 作，1,260 
【大盤指數/股價】47106 / 1340

➢ 2H26 新品逐步放量，營運動能增溫  
雙鴻 8 月營收 31.4 億元，MoM-16.6%，YoY+67.2%， 營收月減是受客
戶拉貨排程影響，以及 7 月基期較高所致。雙鴻 1H26 伺服器營收占比
已突破 70%(水冷營收占比已達 55%)，3Q26 為新舊產品交替期，整體
2H26 在 ASIC 與 GPU 伺服器的強勢帶動下，營運展望樂觀，公司維持
全年營收 YoY+70%的目標不變： 
1)隨著搭載新一代晶片的 AISC 伺服器問世，ASIC 散熱方案預期會在
4Q26~1Q27 明顯推升雙鴻業績 ， 且ASIC 客戶持續擴大水冷散熱導入 ，
此部分效益可挹注到 2027 年；2)雙鴻 3Q26 cold plate 出貨以 GB300
為大宗 ， 近期宣布其Vera Rubin cold plate， 已回到NVIDIA 推薦名單 ，
預期 4Q26 小量出貨；3)其他新品方面，DIMM cold plate 已於 2Q26
小量出貨。CPU 均熱片 2026 年營收占比為個位數，要到 2027~2028
年效益才會較明顯。 
➢ 2027 年泰國持續擴產 
雙鴻生產基地位於台灣、中國(廣州/昆山/重慶/合肥)、泰國，泰國廠為
近年擴產重點 ：1)泰國一期廠區已於 2025 年投產 ， 主要生產cold plate、
manifold、Rack CDU 等液冷散熱元件，目前產能為 cold plate 250k/
月、manifold 3k/ 月；2)泰國二期於 2026 年 9 月正式量產，主要生產
cold plate 、manifold，並預留電源水冷產能，若各產品線產能順利拉
升 ， 未來泰國各項產品產能可翻倍 ；3)泰國三期規劃在 4Q26 動工 、2H27
投產；4)泰國四期預計 2028 年動工。 
➢ 建議調升至買進，目標價 1,620 元  
雙鴻 2H26 營運展望正向，AI 伺服器的強勢增長，能彌補消費性電子市
況低迷帶來的不利影響。 3Q26 GB 300 散熱產品出貨持續增量，並且
cold plate 已重新回到 NVIDIA Vera Rubin 推薦名單，預期 4Q26 小量
出貨，而 ASIC 客戶之新品亦逐漸放量中，有利營運回溫。雙鴻歷史 PE
區間為 8X~36X， 考量新品可逐步帶動2H26~1H27 之業績 ， 且目前PE
評價偏低，建議逢低買進，目標價 1,620 元(2027F PE 20X)。
